<div align="left"><table class="RegBitDef"><col width="112.59px"/><col width="501.58px"/><tr height="90.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 31</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">LOCK</span>: OPAMP4_TCMR lock</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is write-once. It is set by software. It can only be cleared by a system reset.</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is used to configure the OPAMP4_TCMR register as read-only.</p><p class="TableIndent">0: OPAMP4_TCMR is read-write</p><p class="TableIndent">1: OPAMP4_TCMR is read-only</p></td></tr><tr height="22.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 30:6</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL">Reserved, must be kept at reset value.</p></td></tr><tr height="117.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 5</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">T20CM_EN</span>: TIM20 controlled mux mode enable</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is set and cleared by software. It is used to control automatically the switch between the default selection (VP_SEL and VM_SEL) and the secondary selection (VPS_SEL and VMS_SEL) of the inverting and non inverting inputs. This automatic switch is triggered by the TIM20 CC6 output arriving on the OPAMP4 input multiplexers.</p><p class="TableIndent">0: Automatic switch is disabled.</p><p class="TableIndent">1: Automatic switch is enabled.</p></td></tr><tr height="117.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 4</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">T8CM_EN</span>: TIM8 controlled mux mode enable</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is set and cleared by software. It is used to control automatically the switch between the default selection (VP_SEL and VM_SEL) and the secondary selection (VPS_SEL and VMS_SEL) of the inverting and non inverting inputs. This automatic switch is triggered by the TIM8 CC6 output arriving on the OPAMP4 input multiplexers.</p><p class="TableIndent">0: Automatic switch is disabled.</p><p class="TableIndent">1: Automatic switch is enabled.</p></td></tr><tr height="117.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 3</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">T1CM_EN</span>: TIM1 controlled mux mode enable</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is set and cleared by software. It is used to control automatically the switch between the default selection (VP_SEL and VM_SEL) and the secondary selection (VPS_SEL and VMS_SEL) of the inverting and non inverting inputs. This automatic switch is triggered by the TIM1 CC6 output arriving on the OPAMP4 input multiplexers.</p><p class="TableIndent">0: Automatic switch is disabled.</p><p class="TableIndent">1: Automatic switch is enabled.</p></td></tr><tr height="133.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 2:1</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">VPS_SEL[1:0]</span>: OPAMP4 Non inverting input secondary selection.</p><p class="TableIndent">These bits are set and cleared by software. They are used to select the OPAMP4 non inverting input when the controlled mux mode is enabled (T1CM_EN = 1 or T8CM_EN = 1 or T20CM_EN = 1)</p><p class="TableIndent">00: VINP0 pin connected to OPAMP4 VINP input</p><p class="TableIndent">01: VINP1 pin connected to OPAMP4 VINP input</p><p class="TableIndent">10: VINP2 pin connected to OPAMP4 VINP input</p><p class="TableIndent">11: DAC4_CH1 connected to OPAMP4 VINP input</p></td></tr><tr height="156.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 0</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">VMS_SEL</span>: OPAMP4 inverting input secondary selection</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is set and cleared by software. It is used to select the OPAMP4 inverting input when the controlled mux mode is enabled (T1CM_EN = 1 or T8CM_EN = 1 or T20CM_EN = 1).</p><p class="TableBodyL">When standalone mode is used (i.e. VM_SEL = “00” or “01”):</p><p class="TableIndent" style="margin-left : 22.68pt;">0: Input from VINM0</p><p class="TableIndent" style="margin-left : 22.68pt;">1: Input from VINM1</p><p class="TableBodyL">When PGA (VM_SEL = “10”) or Follower mode (VM_SEL = “11”) is used:</p><p class="TableIndent" style="margin-left : 22.68pt;">0: Resistor feedback output selected (PGA mode)</p><p class="TableIndent" style="margin-left : 22.68pt;">1: V<sub>OUT</sub> selected as input minus (follower mode)</p></td></tr></table></div>


### 25.5.11 OPAMP5 timer controlled mode register (OPAMPx\_TCMR) (x = 1...6)

Address offset: 0x28

Reset value: 0x0000 0000

<div align="left"><table class="RegBitDef"><col width="112.59px"/><col width="501.58px"/><tr height="90.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 31</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">LOCK</span>: OPAMP5_TCMR lock</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is write-once. It is set by software. It can only be cleared by a system reset.</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is used to configure the OPAMP5_TCMR register as read-only.</p><p class="TableIndent">0: OPAMP5_TCMR is read-write</p><p class="TableIndent">1: OPAMP5_TCMR is read-only</p></td></tr><tr height="22.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 30:6</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL">Reserved, must be kept at reset value.</p></td></tr><tr height="117.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 5</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">T20CM_EN</span>: TIM20 controlled mux mode enable</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is set and cleared by software. It is used to control automatically the switch between the default selection (VP_SEL and VM_SEL) and the secondary selection (VPS_SEL and VMS_SEL) of the inverting and non inverting inputs. This automatic switch is triggered by the TIM20 CC6 output arriving on the OPAMP5 input multiplexers.</p><p class="TableIndent">0: Automatic switch is disabled.</p><p class="TableIndent">1: Automatic switch is enabled.</p></td></tr><tr height="117.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 4</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">T8CM_EN</span>: TIM8 controlled mux mode enable</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is set and cleared by software. It is used to control automatically the switch between the default selection (VP_SEL and VM_SEL) and the secondary selection (VPS_SEL and VMS_SEL) of the inverting and non inverting inputs. This automatic switch is triggered by the TIM8 CC6 output arriving on the OPAMP5 input multiplexers.</p><p class="TableIndent">0: Automatic switch is disabled.</p><p class="TableIndent">1: Automatic switch is enabled.</p></td></tr><tr height="117.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 3</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">T1CM_EN</span>: TIM1 controlled mux mode enable</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is set and cleared by software. It is used to control automatically the switch between the default selection (VP_SEL and VM_SEL) and the secondary selection (VPS_SEL and VMS_SEL) of the inverting and non inverting inputs. This automatic switch is triggered by the TIM1 CC6 output arriving on the OPAMP5 input multiplexers.</p><p class="TableIndent">0: Automatic switch is disabled.</p><p class="TableIndent">1: Automatic switch is enabled.</p></td></tr><tr height="133.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 2:1</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">VPS_SEL[1:0]</span>: OPAMP5 Non inverting input secondary selection.</p><p class="TableIndent">These bits are set and cleared by software. They are used to select the OPAMP5 non inverting input when the controlled mux mode is enabled (T1CM_EN = 1 or T8CM_EN = 1 or T20CM_EN = 1)</p><p class="TableIndent">00: VINP0 pin connected to OPAMP5 VINP input</p><p class="TableIndent">01: VINP1 pin connected to OPAMP5 VINP input</p><p class="TableIndent">10: VINP2 pin connected to OPAMP5 VINP input</p><p class="TableIndent">11: DAC4_CH2 connected to OPAMP5 VINP input</p></td></tr><tr height="156.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 0</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">VMS_SEL</span>: OPAMP5 inverting input secondary selection</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is set and cleared by software. It is used to select the OPAMP5 inverting input when the controlled mux mode is enabled (T1CM_EN = 1 or T8CM_EN = 1 or T20CM_EN = 1)</p><p class="TableBodyL">When standalone mode is used (i.e. VM_SEL = “00” or “01”):</p><p class="TableIndent" style="margin-left : 22.68pt;">0: Input from VINM0</p><p class="TableIndent" style="margin-left : 22.68pt;">1: Input from VINM1</p><p class="TableBodyL">When PGA (VM_SEL = “10”) or Follower mode (VM_SEL = “11”) is used:</p><p class="TableIndent" style="margin-left : 22.68pt;">0: Resistor feedback output selected (PGA mode)</p><p class="TableIndent" style="margin-left : 22.68pt;">1: V<sub>OUT</sub> selected as input minus (follower mode)</p></td></tr></table></div>


### 25.5.12 OPAMP6 timer controlled mode register (OPAMPx\_TCMR) (x = 1...6)

Address offset: 0x2C

Reset value: 0x0000 0000

<div align="left"><table class="RegBitDef"><col width="112.59px"/><col width="501.58px"/><tr height="90.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 31</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">LOCK</span>: OPAMP6_TCMR lock</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is write-once. It is set by software. It can only be cleared by a system reset.</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is used to configure the OPAMP6_TCMR register as read-only.</p><p class="TableIndent">0: OPAMP6_TCMR is read-write</p><p class="TableIndent">1: OPAMP6_TCMR is read-only</p></td></tr><tr height="22.67px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 30:6</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL">Reserved, must be kept at reset value.</p></td></tr><tr height="117.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 5</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">T20CM_EN</span>: TIM20 controlled mux mode enable</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is set and cleared by software. It is used to control automatically the switch between the default selection (VP_SEL and VM_SEL) and the secondary selection (VPS_SEL and VMS_SEL) of the inverting and non inverting inputs. This automatic switch is triggered by the TIM20 CC6 output arriving on the OPAMP6 input multiplexers.</p><p class="TableIndent">0: Automatic switch is disabled.</p><p class="TableIndent">1: Automatic switch is enabled.</p></td></tr><tr height="117.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 4</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">T8CM_EN</span>: TIM8 controlled mux mode enable</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is set and cleared by software. It is used to control automatically the switch between the default selection (VP_SEL and VM_SEL) and the secondary selection (VPS_SEL and VMS_SEL) of the inverting and non inverting inputs. This automatic switch is triggered by the TIM8 CC6 output arriving on the OPAMP6 input multiplexers.</p><p class="TableIndent">0: Automatic switch is disabled.</p><p class="TableIndent">1: Automatic switch is enabled.</p></td></tr><tr height="117.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 3</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">T1CM_EN</span>: TIM1 controlled mux mode enable</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is set and cleared by software. It is used to control automatically the switch between the default selection (VP_SEL and VM_SEL) and the secondary selection (VPS_SEL and VMS_SEL) of the inverting and non inverting inputs. This automatic switch is triggered by the TIM1 CC6 output arriving on the OPAMP6 input multiplexers.</p><p class="TableIndent">0: Automatic switch is disabled.</p><p class="TableIndent">1: Automatic switch is enabled.</p></td></tr><tr height="133.33px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bits 2:1</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">VPS_SEL[1:0]</span>: OPAMP6 Non inverting input secondary selection.</p><p class="TableIndent">These bits are set and cleared by software. They are used to select the OPAMP6 non inverting input when the controlled mux mode is enabled (T1CM_EN = 1 or T8CM_EN = 1 or T20CM_EN = 1)</p><p class="TableIndent">00: VINP0 pin connected to OPAMP6 VINP input</p><p class="TableIndent">01: VINP1 pin connected to OPAMP6 VINP input</p><p class="TableIndent">10: VINP2 pin connected to OPAMP6 VINP input</p><p class="TableIndent">11: DAC3_CH1 connected to OPAMP6 VINP input</p></td></tr><tr height="156.00px"><td><p class="RegTBodyR">Bit 0</p></td><td><p class="TableBodyL"><span style="font-weight:Bold;">VMS_SEL</span>: OPAMP6 inverting input secondary selection</p><p class="TableBodyL">This bit is set and cleared by software. It is used to select the OPAMP6 inverting input when the controlled mux mode is enabled (T1CM_EN = 1 or T8CM_EN = 1 or T20CM_EN = 1)</p><p class="TableBodyL">When standalone mode is used (i.e. VM_SEL = “00” or “01”):</p><p class="TableIndent" style="margin-left : 22.68pt;">0: Input from VINM0</p><p class="TableIndent" style="margin-left : 22.68pt;">1: Input from VINM1</p><p class="TableBodyL">When PGA (VM_SEL = “10”) or Follower mode (VM_SEL = “11”) is used:</p><p class="TableIndent" style="margin-left : 22.68pt;">0: Resistor feedback output selected (PGA mode)</p><p class="TableIndent" style="margin-left : 22.68pt;">1: V<sub>OUT</sub> selected as input minus (follower mode)</p></td></tr></table></div>
